Let's design robust final program:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=2400):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0,budget_s-10)
    global_best = inf; global_a=None
    # candidate lengths e.g. [8192,16384] maybe if budget >1200
    for n in lengths:
        run_pipeline(n, time allocation)
    # final verify and return best global_a
```

Core functions:
- `fft_conv(a, N)` returns c (linear conv) and A maybe. For a length n, pad N. Use `scipy.fft.fft` or `numpy.fft.fft`. SciPy maybe faster. Use double. To enforce real: `np.real(ifft(A*A))[:2*n-1]`. But c may have tiny imaginary. Also sum c = S^2? numerical.

```
import time, math
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft

def project_simplex(v, S): ...
def safe_normalize(a,n,S): ...
def compute_conv(a,N):
   c = np.zeros(2*len(a)-1)
   A = fft(a, N)
   c[...] = np.real(ifft(A*A))[:2*len(a)-1]
   return c, A
# maybe compute A separately
def lse_grad(a,N,beta):
   c,A = compute_conv(a,N)
   cmax = c.max()
   e = np.exp(np.minimum(beta*(c-cmax), 0.0))
   s = e.sum(); if s<=0: w = onehot(argmax)
   else w=e/s
   W = fft(w,N)
   grad = 2*np.real(ifft(W*np.conj(A)))[:n]
   return grad,c,cmax,w
```

Spectral target init function:
```
def make_target_shape(n, S, target_max, rng):
   max_idx = 2*n-2
   kk = np.arange(2*n-1)
   tri = np.minimum(kk+1, 2*n-1-kk).astype(float)
   tri /= n
   tri *= S*S
   c = np.minimum(tri, target_max)
   deficit = S*S - c.sum()
   if deficit > 0:
      room = np.maximum(target_max - c, 0)
      if room.sum()>0:
          c += deficit * room / room.sum()
      else:
          # scale down? cannot if max target low; set c=tri?
   else: # if deficit negative (target shape sum > S2? target max lower than tri sum? cannot lower sum below S2 while max≤target if target<1? For target 1.4 and average~1 ok; cap only reduces sum, deficit positive.
   # optional random modulations preserving max:
   extra = (target_max - c) * rng.random(2*n-1) * 0.1
   if sum: c += extra * deficit? Hmm.
   return np.maximum(c,0)
```

Since original total S2, deficit = S2 - capped_sum = sum over capped (tri-target). Room over uncapped = target* (#uncapped) - truncated_sum. Is room.sum always ≥ deficit? Total room = target*(2n-1) - capped_sum. Need deficit = S2 - capped_sum. room.sum - deficit = target*(2n-1) - S2 = (2n-1)target - 2n ≈ target. For target 1.5, positive ~? (2n-1)*1.5 -2n =0.5n-1.5 >0. So yes. Good. For target near1, target*(2n-1)-2n≈ -n, room insufficient if target<~1. Average >target impossible. So target must > average ~1. Good.
- Add random perturbation: To diversify, we can set target_max maybe 1.45-1.6. Use rng.normal on c but clip to [0,target]. def make_conj_sym(q):
```
    q = np.asarray(q)
    if q.size %2 ==0:
       r = q[0].real + 0j
       nq = q[-1].real + 0j
       out = np.empty_like(q)
       out[0]=r; out[-1]= q[-1].real
       out[1:-1] = np.conj(q[-2:0:-1])
    else:
       out = np.empty_like(q); out[0]=q[0].real; out[1:] = np.conj(q[-1:0:-1])
    return out
```

Let's define `spectral_target_step(a,n,S,N,L, rng)`:
- compute c and cmax from a.
- `c_target = cap_and_redistribute(c, L, S*S)` where L maybe target max. Starting from current c helps maintain continuity. This is better than base triangular: flatten current peaks and redistribute to low lags. This is like direct time-domain shaping. If L >= current cmax, no change. If L too low such that feasible max < target? Ensure target > average? If L < S*S/(2n-1)+small impossible; but we only use L >1.01 maybe.
   - `c_mod = c.copy(); over = c_mod > L; c_mod[over]=L; deficit = S*S - c_mod.sum(); if deficit>0: room = L - c_mod; room = np.maximum(room,0); if room.sum() >0: c_mod += deficit*room/room.sum(); else c_mod = clip?` But adding to low lags may make some exactly L. Good.
   - If deficit<0 (c sum > S2 due to numerical), scale down? `c_mod *= S*S/c_mod.sum()`.
- Optionally add small random perturbation to c_target within [0,L] to escape. E.g., `noise = rng.normal(0, 0.01*L, size)`; `c_mod = clip(c_mod+noise,0,L); renormalize with redistribution`.
- Factor to a candidate.
- project and return.

Base target function top-level:
```
def make_base_target(n,S,L,rng):
   kk=np.arange(2*n-1)
   tri = np.minimum(kk+1, 2*n-1-kk).astype(float) / float(n) * (S*S)
   c = np.minimum(tri, L)
   deficit = S*S - c.sum()
   room = np.maximum(L - c, 0)
   if room.sum()>0:
      c += deficit*room/room.sum()
   else: # fallback uniform? c = np.full(2n-1, S*S/(2n-1))
   # random perturb
   if rng: ...
   c = np.clip(c,0,L)
   # ensure sum with redistribution
   for few: if sum != S2, adjust low lags
   return c
```
Ensure target sum exactly S2. After adding to room, due to floating may small. We can adjust: `diff=S2-c.sum(); if abs(diff)>... add to lags with room proportional`. But if room zero and diff positive impossible (L too low). Set fallback `c.fill(S2/(2n-1))` (max ~1). But target L > average.

Let's design `lbfgs_block` with parameters: max_time, beta_start, beta_max, m=10, noise_scale=0.0, max_iter maybe limited by time.

Pseudo:
```
def lbfgs_block(a_init,n,S,N,max_time,rng,beta_start=5,beta_max=200,m=10,verbose=False):
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    a=safe_normalize(a_init,n,S)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_cmax=compute_conv(a,N)[0].max()
    beta=beta_start
    g,c,cmax,w=lse_grad(a,N,beta)
    f = lse_value(c,beta)
    s_hist=[]; y_hist=[]
    step_base = 2.0*S/(n*max(beta,1.0))
    no_progress=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()-t_start < max_time:
       it+=1
       if it % 50 ==0:
          beta = min(beta_max, beta*1.05)
          # clear history when beta changes significantly
          s_hist.clear(); y_hist.clear()
          g,c,cmax,w = lse_grad(a,N,beta)
          f = lse_value(c,beta)
          step_base=2.0*S/(n*max(beta,1.0))
          if cmax < best_cmax - 1e-9: best...
       dir = lbfgs_dir(s_hist,y_hist,g)
       dirn = norm(dir)
       if dirn ==0: break
       # scale dir so initial change ~step_base
       if dirn > step_base: dir *= step_base/dirn
       # line search
       alpha=1.0; accept=False
       for ls in range(8):
           cand=project_simplex(a+alpha*dir,S)
           c_ls,_,? = compute_conv(cand,N)
           cmax_ls=c_ls.max()
           f_ls=lse_value(c_ls,beta)
           # accept if f_ls <= f - 1e-4*alpha*np.dot(dir,g) or cmax_ls < cmax
           if f_ls <= f - 1e-5*alpha*max(dot,0) or cmax_ls <= cmax + 1e-10:
               accept=True; break
           alpha*=0.5
       # If not accept maybe take smallest alpha? We'll just accept last if f improved?
       if accept:
           a_new=cand; c_new...
       else:
           # try smaller fixed perturbation? break?
           # take smallest alpha cand even if not accepted? maybe if cmax lower than best?
           a_new=cand; ...
       if cmax_new < best_cmax: best...
       # update history
       g_new,... = lse_grad(a_new,N,beta)
       s_new=a_new-a; y_new=g_new-g
       if dot(s_new,y_new)>1e-14*dot(s_new,s_new): append
       a=cand; g=g_new; c=c_new; cmax=cmax_new; f=f_new
       if it%100==0 and rng.random()<0.1:
           noise = rng.normal(0, 0.002*S/np.sqrt(n), n)
           a=project_simplex(a+noise,S); clear; recompute g
           # clear no progress
       # periodic evaluate? cmax available
    return best_a,best_cmax
```
Need dir scaling and line search alpha. If dir scaled to step_base, alpha=1 initial max change. But if direction already smaller, fine. For L-BFGS unnormalized, if we limit norm to step_base, initial change ≤step_base. Good.

Line search accept: We may accept if f_ls <= f - 1e-4*alpha*(-dot_dir_g) or cmax_ls < cmax. If gradient small, dot maybe near0, then accept if f <= f (any). Good. But if f_ls not computed? We'll compute `lse_value(c,beta)`.

```
def factor_spectrum(n,N,c,rng,flip_p=0.5):
```

Spectral block algorithm with target L:
```
def spectral_block(a_init,n,S,N,max_time,rng,target_lower=1.45,target_upper=1.51):
    a=safe_normalize(...); best...
    current_cmax=...
    L = min(target_upper, max(target_lower, current_cmax - 0.05))
    stall=0
    start_time=...
    while time - start < max_time:
       candidates = target_candidates(a,n,S,N,L,rng, use_base=(stall>3))
       best_cand=None; best_cand_obj=inf
       for cand in candidates:
           c=compute_conv(cand,N)[0]; val=c.max(); if val<best_cand_obj: ...
       if best_cand is not None:
           if best_cand_obj < best_obj:
               accept; best...
               L=max(target_lower, best_obj - 0.03)
               stall=0
           elif best_cand_obj < current_cmax: # intermediate
               a=best_cand; current_cmax=best_cand_obj; L=max(target_lower, current_cmax - 0.02)
           else:
               stall +=1
               L = min(min(current_cmax, target_upper), L + 0.02 + 0.01*stall)
               if L > current_cmax: L=current_cmax-0.01
               # if too high, use current
               if cand not better: a unchanged
       # periodic random base target even if not stall
    return best_a,best_obj
```
Need `target_candidates` top-level returns list. It will compute c target and factor. It may be time-consuming (FFT c, sqrt, IFFT). 2 candidates per attempt. If each ~several transforms, okay for 300s (maybe hundreds iterations). Good.

Let's implement `redistribute_target(c,S2,L)`:
```
def redistribute_target_c(c,S2,L):
   c = np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64).copy()
   c = np.clip(c,0,L)
   for _ in range(5):
       diff = S2 - c.sum()
       if abs(diff) < 1e-10: break
       if diff >0:
          room = L - c
          room = np.maximum(room,0)
          rsum = room.sum()
          if rsum <= 1e-15:
              # cannot; reduce L? set c = S2/(2n-1) if <=L? fallback
              c.fill(S2 / c.size)
              break
          c += diff * room / rsum
       else:
          # need remove from largest values but keep nonnegative
          scale = S2 / c.sum()
          c *= scale
       c = np.clip(c,0,L)
   # final tiny adjust: if sum different, add/sub to middle? Not needed.
   if c.sum() < 1e-15: c.fill(1)
   return c
```
But if we scale down for diff<0, max≤L still if all multiplied by <1. Good.
If diff>0 and room zero, fallback uniform only if uniform≤L; else impossible. For L>average, uniform≤L. So set c.fill(avg). Good.

`base_target_c(n,S2,L,rng,amp=0)`:

Uniform a_i=S/n. c_k = T_k*(S/n)^2 = S2/n * T_k since S2/n =? S2*(1/n). Yes. Sum T=n^2, c sum=S2/n*n^2=S2*n? Wait S2? Let's check: S2=2n. n=4, T=[1,2,3,4,3,2,1] sum16=n^2. S2/n=8/4=2; c=[2,4,6,8,6,4,2] sum32 = S2? S2=8. Mist! S/n = sqrt(2n)/n = sqrt(2)/sqrt(n); square =2/n. For n=4 square=0.5, S2/n=8/4=2? Wait S2/n = 8/4=2, but (S/n)^2 = S2/n^2? Because (S/n)^2=S2/n^2, not S2/n. Correct: c_k = T_k*(S/n)^2 = S2/n^2 * T_k. Sum T=n^2 => sum=S2. Good. So base tri = T_k * S2 / (n*n). For normalized target max uniform=2 at center T=n => S2/n=2. `target_candidates`:
```
def target_candidates(a,n,S,N,L,rng,use_base,flip_p=0.7):
    S2=S*S
    cands=[]
    if use_base:
        c = make_base_target(n,S2,L,rng, amp=0.05)
    else:
        c,_ = compute_conv(a,N); c = cap_current...
    # maybe add small random noise
    if rng is not None:
        noise = rng.normal(0, 0.02*L, c.size)
        c2 = redistribute_target_c(c + noise, S2, L)
        cands? use c or c2? Maybe choose c if use_current, c2 if stochastic.
    A_list = factor_candidates_from_c(c,n,S,N,rng,flip_p)
```

```
def factor_candidates_from_c(c,n,S,N,rng,flip_p=0.7,n_flips=1):
    Q = fft(c,N)
    Q = make_conj_sym(Q)
    if N %2 ==0: Q[N//2] = abs(Q[N//2])
    A = np.sqrt(Q)
    cands=[]
    # principal and maybe flipped
    for try_idx in range(n_flips):
        A_copy = A.copy()
        if flip_p>0:
            A_copy = apply_random_flips(A_copy, rng, flip_p)
        t = np.real(ifft(A_copy))
        s = best_window_start(t,n)
        seg = get_window(t,s,n)
        a1=project_simplex(np.maximum(seg,0),S)
        a2=project_simplex(np.abs(seg),S)
        # maybe if sum of seg very low, a meaningless
        cands.append(a1); cands.append(a2)
        # maybe also start0
        s0=0; seg0=...
    return cands
```

```
def apply_random_flips(A, rng, p):
    N = A.size; mid=N//2
    A = A.copy()
    if mid > 1 and p>0:
        idx = np.arange(1, mid) if N%2==0 else np.arange(1, mid+1?)
```
For even N, pairs k=1..mid-1. For odd N, k=1..mid. We only use N power of two even. So even.

```
idx = np.arange(1, N//2)
if idx.size:
   flips = rng.random(idx.size) < p
   k = idx[flips]
   if k.size:
       A[k] = -A[k]
       A[N-k] = -A[N-k]
return A
```

Now L-BFGS block with no closures. Need pass many args. Let's implement helper top-level `lse_value(c,beta)`.

```
def lse_value(c,beta):
    cmax = c.max()
    x = np.exp(np.minimum(beta*(c-cmax),0.0))
    logsum = np.log(x.sum())
    return cmax + logsum/beta
```
For beta=0? We always >0.

`lse_grad(c? a,N,beta)`:
```
def lse_grad(a,N,beta):
   c,A = compute_conv(a,N)
   cmax = c.max()
   e = np.exp(np.minimum(beta*(c-cmax),0.0))
   s = e.sum()
   if s < 1e-300:
       w = np.zeros_like(c); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
   else:
       w = e/s
   W = fft(w,N)
   grad = 2.0*np.real(ifft(W*np.conj(A)))[:len(a)]
   f = cmax + np.log(s)/beta
   return grad,c,cmax,w,f
```
If beta small (e.g. 1), fine. If c includes negative small, cmax positive.

Potential issue: For high beta, e sum maybe number top; log(s) positive; f = cmax + log(#top)/beta > cmax. The gradient weights sum1. Good.

`lbfgs_block` code. Important: no closures; local variables fine. Use top-level `time`.

Pseudo in detail:
```
def lbfgs_block(a_init,n,S,N,max_time,rng,beta_start=5.0,beta_max=200.0,m_lbfgs=10,perturb_prob=0.05):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    a=safe_normalize(a_init,n,S)
    c,A=compute_conv(a,N); cmax=c.max()
    best_a=a.copy(); best_cmax=cmax
    beta=beta_start
    g,c,cmax,w,f = lse_grad(a,N,beta) # recompute c maybe duplicates but okay
    s_hist=[]; y_hist=[]
    step_base = 2.0*S/(n*max(beta,1.0))
    it=0; fails=0
    g_small=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < max_time and it < 1000000:
       it+=1
       # ramp beta every 100 iters
       if it % 100 == 0:
          beta_new = min(beta_max, beta*1.05)
          if beta_new != beta:
             beta=beta_new
             s_hist=[]; y_hist=[]
             g,c,cmax,w,f=lse_grad(a,N,beta)
          step_base=2.0*S/(n*max(beta,1.0))
          if cmax < best_cmax - 1e-10:
             best_cmax=cmax; best_a=a.copy()
       dirn = lbfgs_dir(s_hist,y_hist,g)
       dn = np.linalg.norm(dirn)
       if dn < 1e-20:
          break
       # ensure descent
       dg = np.dot(dirn,g)
       if dg > 0:
          dirn = -g
          dn = np.linalg.norm(dirn)
          if dn<1e-20: break
          dg = -np.dot(dirn,g)
       # scale initial change to step_base
       if dn > step_base:
          dirn *= step_base/dn
       # line search
       a_new=a; c_ls=None; cmax_ls=cmax; f_ls=f; ok=False
       alpha=1.0
       armijo_c=1e-4
       for ls in range(10):
          cand = project_simplex(a + alpha*dirn, S)
          c_ls,_ = compute_conv(cand,N)
          cmax_ls = c_ls.max()
          f_ls = lse_value(c_ls,beta)
          if f_ls <= f - armijo_c*alpha*max(0.0,-dg) or cmax_ls <= cmax + 1e-10:
             ok=True; break
          alpha *= 0.5
          if alpha < 1e-6*step_base: break
       a_new = cand; c_new=c_ls; cmax_new=cmax_ls; f_new=f_ls
       if cmax_new < best_cmax - 1e-12:
          best_cmax=cmax_new; best_a=a_new.copy()
       # update history if ok and curvature
       g_new,... = lse_grad(a_new,N,beta)
       s_new=a_new-a; y_new=g_new-g
       sy=np.dot(s_new,y_new); ss=np.dot(s_new,s_new)
       if ok and sy > 1e-14*ss and ss>1e-30:
          s_hist.append(s_new); y_hist.append(y_new)
          if len(s_hist)>m_lbfgs:
             del s_hist[0]; del y_hist[0]
       a=a_new; c=c_new; cmax=cmax_new; f=f_new; g=g_new
       # if f not decreasing often, perturb
       if it % 100 == 0 and best_cmax - cmax < 1e-8 maybe? Actually we want stall.
       noimprove = (best_cmax - cmax) ? track last_best.
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, budget_s - 15.0)
```

```
def factor_candidates(c,n,S,N,rng,flip_p,n_modes=1):
   Q=fft(c,N); sym; abs mid; A=sqrt(...)
   out=[]
   for trial in range(n_modes):
      A2=A.copy()
      if flip_p>0: A2=apply_random_flips(A2,rng,flip_p)
      t=real(ifft(A2))
      start=best_window_start(t,n)
      seg=get_window(t,start,n)
      # candidate1 clip
      a1=project_simplex(np.maximum(seg,0),S)
      out.append(a1)
      a2=project_simplex(np.abs(seg),S)
      # choose one with larger sum? both sum S. Maybe include both
      out.append(a2)
   return out
```

Pseudo spectral_block:
```
def spectral_block(a_init,n,S,N,max_time,rng,target_lower=1.45,target_upper=1.51):
    start=time.monotonic(); end=start+max_time
    a=safe_normalize(...)
    c,A=compute_conv(a,N); cmax=float(c.max())
    best_a=a.copy(); best_obj=cmax
    L = min(target_upper, max(target_lower, cmax - 0.03))
    stall=0
    use_base = False
    it=0
    while time < end:
      it+=1
      cands = target_candidates(a,n,S,N,c,L,rng,use_base,0.7)
      best_cand=None; best_cand_obj=inf
      for cand in cands:
         c2,_=compute_conv(cand,N); obj=float(c2.max())
         if obj < best_cand_obj:
            best_cand_obj=obj; best_cand=cand; best_c=c2
      if best_cand is None: break
      improved_global = best_cand_obj < best_obj - 1e-10
      if improved_global:
          best_a=best_cand.copy(); best_obj=best_cand_obj
          a=best_cand.copy(); c=best_c; cmax=best_cand_obj
          L = max(target_lower, best_obj - 0.02 - 0.005*stall?); stall=0
          use_base=False
      elif best_cand_obj < cmax - 1e-10:
          # progress not global
          a=best_cand.copy(); c=best_c; cmax=best_cand_obj
          L = max(target_lower, cmax - 0.01)
          stall=0
          use_base=False
      else:
          stall +=1
          # increase L
          L = min(min(cmax, target_upper), L + 0.02 + 0.01*stall)
          if L >= cmax: L = max(target_lower, cmax - 0.01)
          if stall % 3 == 0:
              use_base = True
          if stall > 10:
              # random restart from current with noise
              a = project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0,0.005*S/np.sqrt(n),n),S)
              c,_=compute_conv(a,N); cmax=c.max()
              L = min(target_upper, max(target_lower, cmax - 0.05))
              stall=0
              use_base=True
      # occasional base target even if good
      if it % 50 == 0 and rng.random()<0.3:
          use_base=True
      else:
          # after evaluating, reset use_base? Could keep.
          pass
    return best_a,best_obj
```
Need if use_base remains true many iterations; set false when improved. The `target_candidates` uses c_current if not base; if use_base, use base. After base attempt fails, next should current. Maybe `use_base` per attempt: choose base if stall≥3 or rng<0.1. Don't keep variable too long. We'll set `use_base = (stall>=3) or (rng.random()<0.1)`.

Could `best_window_start` O(N) with prefix be slow for each candidate? It uses cumsum length 2N; each candidate. For 4 candidates, memory/time. Maybe use peak-based O(1) for speed. Let's implement peak-based plus maybe one max window if N not huge? Use O(1):
```
def best_window_start(t,n):
    # choose start such that a large positive peak is centered; also consider start where mean window high via rough? We'll use peak.
    val_max = np.max(t)
    if val_max <= 0: return 0
    idx = int(np.argmax(t))
    s_center = (n//2 - idx) % len(t)
    s_peak = idx % len(t)
    # evaluate a few candidate starts by window sum quickly O(1) using prefix? We can precompute prefix? no.
    # just return s_center
    return s_center
```
But if support is not peaked (flat), argmax random; centering maybe okay. Could use max sum via cumulative once per factor call, not per candidate? We can do O(N) because factor call count moderate. Let's keep O(N) to improve candidate quality. Implement efficient:
```
def best_window_start(t,n):
    N=len(t)
    if n<=0: return 0
    if n>=N: return 0
    tt = np.empty(2*N, dtype=np.float64)
    tt[:N]=t; tt[N:]=t
    ps = np.empty(2*N+1); ps[0]=0; ps[1:]=np.cumsum(tt)
    vals = ps[n:n+N] - ps[:N]  # length N? ps length 2N+1. For s=N-1: ps[n+N-1]-ps[N-1] max index n+N-1 <2N? yes if n<=N.
    s=int(np.argmax(vals))
    return s % N
```
Memory ~2N+1*8 ~1MB for N=32768, okay. But doing for each candidate 4 times allocate. Could integrate: In `factor_candidates`, for each A/t, call; okay if iterations hundreds. 1000*4*1MB allocation=4GB memory churn but freed; could be slow. Use peak-based to avoid. Maybe hybrid: if N <= 16384 use max window else peak. For n=8192 N=16384, O(N) 128KB, fine; 16384 N=32768 256KB/tt? 2N float 512KB, okay but alloc. Could still okay. 1000 alloc*4=2000*512KB=1GB, not bad. Cumsum C optimized. Let's use O(N) for quality.

Linear convolution length 2n-1 plus zero at index 2n-1. `ifft(A*A)` length N returns cyclic convolution. For indices 0..2n-2, since N=2n, there is no wrap overlap for lags <2n-1? Need check: cyclic conv c_k = sum_{i+j ≡ k mod 2n} a_i a_j. For k=0, terms i+j=0 and i+j=2n. i,j≤n-1, max sum 2n-2 <2n, so no wrap. For k=2n-1, terms i+j=2n-1 (none because max 2n-2) and i+j=0 (a0^2?) Wait cyclic at k=N-1 gets i+j=N (none) or i+j=N-1 (max 2n-1? max 2n-2, so none?) Actually N=2n, k=N-1=2n-1, terms i+j=2n-1 none. Linear convolution length 2n-1 indices 0..2n-2; index 2n-1 zero. So first 2n-1 correct. Good.

```
def init_alternating(n,rng):
   i=np.arange(n)
   a = 1.0 + 0.7*np.cos(np.pi*i + rng.random()*0.2)  # period 2
   a += 0.15*np.sin(2*np.pi*(3*i)/n + rng.random()*2*np.pi)
   return np.clip(a,0.05,None)
```

Let's write each carefully.

`clean_array`:
```
def clean_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = a.copy()
    bad = ~np.isfinite(a)
    if np.any(bad):
        a[bad] = 0.0
    return a
```

`safe_normalize`:
```
def safe_normalize(a,n,S):
    a = clean_array(a)
    if a.shape[0] != n:
        a = np.resize(a,n) # maybe not ideal
    a = np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-15:
       return np.full(n,S/float(n),dtype=np.float64)
    a = a*(S/s)
    a = np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    # after clip sum may change; project? use project_simplex to restore
    if not np.isfinite(a).all(): a.fill(1.0)
    return project_simplex(a,S)
```

`project_simplex`:
```
def project_simplex(v,S):
    v = np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size
    if n ==0: return v
    if S <=0: return np.zeros(n)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cs = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=np.float64)
    valid = (cs - S) <= w*idx
    if np.any(valid):
       rho = float(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]) + 1.0) # but need int
    else:
       rho = float(n)
```
Need rho int for indexing. Better:
```
valid_idx = np.where(valid)[0]
if valid_idx.size >0:
    r = int(valid_idx[-1]) + 1
else: r = n
theta = (cs[r-1] - S)/r
u = np.maximum(v - theta,0)
s = u.sum(); if s<=0: u.fill(S/n) else: u*=S/s
return u
```
Use `np.where(valid)[0]` returns array. If none. Good. For large n, O(n). `cs` float64.
Potential numeric: valid condition `(cs - S) <= w[idx]*idx`. For large values, okay. If S very small vs v, valid maybe. Good.

`compute_conv`:
```
def compute_conv(a,N):
    n=a.size
    A=fft(a,N)
    c=np.real(ifft(A*A))[:2*n-1]
    return c,A
```
No clean? assume.

`lse_weights_f`:
```
def lse_weights_f(c,beta):
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size>0 else 0.0
    if beta <= 0: return np.ones(c.size)/c.size, cmax, cmax? not.
    diff = beta*(c-cmax)
    diff = np.minimum(diff, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(diff)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s < 1e-300:
       w = np.zeros_like(c,dtype=np.float64)
       if c.size: w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
       f = cmax
    else:
       w = e/s
       f = cmax + np.log(s)/beta
    return w,f,cmax
```
If c all negative? cmax negative? But c nonneg mostly. If cmax negative and beta positive, fine. f maybe. For optimal c around1.

`lse_grad_from_a_cA`:
```
def lse_grad_from_a_cA(a,c,A,N,beta):
    w,f,cmax = lse_weights_f(c,beta)
    W = fft(w,N)
    grad = 2.0*np.real(ifft(W*np.conj(A)))[:a.size]
    return grad,w,f,cmax
```
W length N.

`lse_grad`:
```
def lse_grad(a,N,beta):

    return lse_grad_from_a_cA(a,c,A,N,beta)
```

`lbfgs_dir`: Ensure if m small. Code:
```
def lbfgs_dir(s_hist, y_hist, g):
    m=len(s_hist); n=g.size
    gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
    if m ==0 or gn <1e-300:
       if gn <1e-300: return np.zeros(n)
       return -g/gn
    q = g.copy()
    alphas = np.zeros(m)
    for i in range(m-1,-1,-1):
       s_i = s_hist[i]; y_i=y_hist[i]
       yy=float(np.dot(y_i,y_i))
       if yy <1e-30: alphas[i]=0.0
       else:
          alphas[i]=float(np.dot(s_i,q))/yy
          q = q - alphas[i]*y_i
    s_last=s_hist[-1]; y_last=y_hist[-1]
    yy_last=float(np.dot(y_last,y_last))
    if yy_last >1e-30:
       sy_last=float(np.dot(s_last,y_last))
       gamma = max(1e-12, sy_last/yy_last)
    else: gamma=1.0
    r = gamma*q
    for i in range(m):
       s_i=s_hist[i]; y_i=y_hist[i]
       yy=float(np.dot(y_i,y_i))
       if yy<1e-30: continue
       beta_i=float(np.dot(s_i,y_i))/yy
       r = r + (alphas[i] - beta_i)*y_i
    direction = -r
    if float(np.dot(g,direction)) > 0:
       direction = -g
    return direction
```
If direction all zeros, return -g/gn? We'll handle in block.

```
def factor_candidates(c,n,S,N,rng,flip_p=0.7):
    Q=fft(c,N); Q=make_conj_sym(Q); mid...; A=np.sqrt(Q)
    out=[]
    # mode principal
    t=np.real(ifft(A))
    s=best_window_start(t,n)
    seg=get_window(t,s,n)
    out.append(project_simplex(np.maximum(seg,0),S))
    out.append(project_simplex(np.abs(seg),S))
    # mode flipped if flip_p>0
    if flip_p>0:
        A2=apply_random_flips(A,rng,flip_p)
        t2=np.real(ifft(A2))
        s2=best_window_start(t2,n)
        seg2=get_window(t2,s2,n)
        out.append(project_simplex(np.maximum(seg2,0),S))
        # maybe abs flipped? Could be 4. Add abs maybe.
        out.append(project_simplex(np.abs(seg2),S))
    return out
```
4 candidates. Good.

Let's write `cap_redistribute` carefully:
```
def cap_redistribute(c,S2,L):
    c = np.array(c, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    if L <=0: c.fill(max(0,S2/c.size)); return c
    c = np.clip(c,0.0,L)
    for _ in range(6):
        diff = S2 - float(np.sum(c))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-8*max(1.0,S2): break
        if diff > 0:
            room = L - c
            room = np.maximum(room, 0.0)
            rsum = float(np.sum(room))
            if rsum <= 1e-15:
                c.fill(S2 / float(c.size))
                break
            c += diff * room / rsum
        else:
            # reduce proportionally, preserving nonneg
            c *= S2 / max(float(np.sum(c)), 1e-300)
        c = np.clip(c,0.0,L)
    # final scale if sum not exact? If sum < S2 and no room, fill avg
    s=float(np.sum(c))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        c.fill(max(0.0, S2/c.size))
    elif abs(s-S2)>1e-8*max(1.0,S2):
        # small adjust on largest room
        diff=S2-s
        if diff>0:
           room=L-c; room=np.maximum(room,0); if room.sum()>0: c += diff*room/room.sum()
        else:
           c *= S2/s
        c=np.clip(c,0,L)
    return c
```
If L too low such that uniform avg >L, c.fill(avg) violates L. But target_lower > avg. If not, cap_redistribute will leave max >L? We clip to L, sum maybe lower; fallback fill avg but if avg>L then violates. We can ensure L>=avg in spectral block by `L = max(L, S2/c.size + 1e-6)`. Good. In `make_base_target` also.

`make_base_target`:
```
def make_base_target(n,S2,L,rng,amp=0.0):
    avg = S2/(2*n-1)
    L = max(L, avg+1e-12)
    kk = np.arange(2*n-1, dtype=np.float64)
    tri = np.minimum(kk+1.0, (2*n-1.0)-kk)  # for kk 0..2n-2, right = 2n-1-kk
    tri *= S2 / float(n*n)
    c = np.minimum(tri, L)
    if amp>0:
       noise = rng.normal(0.0, amp*L, c.size)
       c = c + noise
    c = cap_redistribute(c,S2,L)
    return c
```
Noise may create negative; cap handles. For deterministic, use rng. If amp small.

`target_candidates`:
```
def target_candidates(a,n,S,N,c_current,L,rng,use_base,flip_p=0.7):
    S2=S*S
    avg=S2/(2*n-1)
    L=max(L, avg+1e-12)
    out=[]
    if use_base:
       ctarget = make_base_target(n,S2,L,rng,amp=0.05)
    else:
       ctarget = cap_redistribute(c_current,S2,L)
       if rng is not None and rng.random()<0.4:
           noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.02*L, ctarget.size)
           ctarget = cap_redistribute(ctarget+noise,S2,L)
    cands = factor_candidates(ctarget,n,S,N,rng,flip_p)
    out.extend(cands)
    return out
```
Maybe use_base base target can be with different L; fine.

`spectral_block`: Use `target_candidates`. In each iter, evaluate candidates. To save, if cands empty break.
```
def spectral_block(a_init,n,S,N,max_time,rng,target_lower=1.45,target_upper=1.51):
   if max_time <=0: return safe_normalize...
   end=...
   a=safe_normalize
   c,A=compute_conv; cmax=...
   best...
   L = min(target_upper, max(target_lower, cmax - 0.04))
   stall=0; it=0
   while time<end:
      it+=1
      use_base = (stall >= 3) or (rng.random()<0.12)
      cands = target_candidates(a,n,S,N,c,L,rng,use_base,0.7)
      best_cand=None; best_obj_iter=inf; best_c_iter=None
      for cand in cands:
          c2,A2=compute_conv(cand,N)
          obj=float(np.max(c2))
          if obj < best_obj_iter:
             best_obj_iter=obj; best_cand=cand; best_c_iter=c2
      if best_cand is None: break
      if best_obj_iter < best_obj - 1e-10:
         best...
         a=best_cand.copy(); c=best_c_iter.copy(); cmax=best_obj_iter
         L=max(target_lower, min(target_upper, best_obj - 0.025 - 0.005*min(stall,5)))
         stall=0
      elif best_obj_iter < cmax - 1e-10:
         a=best_cand.copy(); c=best_c_iter.copy(); cmax=best_obj_iter
         L=max(target_lower, min(target_upper, cmax - 0.015))
         stall=0
      else:
         stall +=1
         L = min(min(cmax, target_upper), L + 0.02 + 0.01*stall)
         if L >= cmax: L=max(target_lower, cmax - 0.01)
         if stall > 8:
             a=project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0,0.005*S/np.sqrt(n),n),S)

      if it % 40 == 0 and best_obj < target_upper +0.02:
          # maybe do nothing
       pass
   return best_a,best_obj
```

`interpolate_seq`:
```
def interpolate_seq(seq,n):
   seq=clean_array(seq)
   if seq.size == n: return seq.copy()
   if seq.size <2: return np.full(n, float(np.mean(seq)) if seq.size>0 else 0.0)
   old = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,seq.size)
   new = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   return np.interp(new, old, seq)
```

`build_inits` top-level:
```
def build_inits(n,S,N,rng,given_seq,extra_seq,need_spectral=True):
    inits=[]
    inits.append(np.full(n,S/float(n),dtype=np.float64))
    inits.append(init_rand(n,rng))
    inits.append(init_chirp(n,rng))
    inits.append(init_lowfreq(n,rng))
    inits.append(init_fourier(n,rng,N))
    if need_spectral:
        inits.append(init_spectral_target(n,S,N,rng,1.50))
    if given_seq is not None:
       inits.append(interpolate_seq(given_seq,n))
    if extra_seq is not None:
       inits.append(interpolate_seq(extra_seq,n))
    return inits
```

```
def apply_random_flips(A,rng,p):
    N=A.size
    if p <=0 or N<4: return A
    A2=A.copy()
    mid=N//2
    idx=np.arange(1,mid)
    if idx.size:
       flips = rng.random(idx.size) < p
       k=idx[flips]
       if k.size:
          A2[k] = -A2[k]
          A2[N-k] = -A2[N-k]
    return A2
```

```
def best_window_start(t,n):
```

```
def lbfgs_block(a_init,n,S,N,max_time,rng,beta_start=5.0,beta_max=200.0,m_lbfgs=10):
    a = safe_normalize(a_init,n,S)
    c,A = compute_conv(a,N)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    best_a = a.copy(); best_obj = cmax
    if max_time <= 0:
        return best_a,best_obj
    t_end = time.monotonic()+max_time
    beta = float(beta_start)
    g,w,f,cmax = lse_grad_from_a_cA(a,c,A,N,beta)
    s_hist=[]; y_hist=[]
    step_base = 2.0*S/(n*max(beta,1.0))
    last_best=best_obj; stall=0; rejects=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        it += 1
        if it % 100 == 0:
            beta = min(float(beta_max), beta*1.06)
            step_base = 2.0*S/(n*max(beta,1.0))
            s_hist=[]; y_hist=[]
            # recompute g with new beta using existing c,A
            g,w,f,cmax = lse_grad_from_a_cA(a,c,A,N,beta)
            if cmax < best_obj - 1e-12:
                best_obj = cmax; best_a=a.copy(); last_best=best_obj
        dirn = lbfgs_dir(s_hist,y_hist,g)
        dn = float(np.linalg.norm(dirn))
        if dn < 1e-20:
            gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
            if gn < 1e-20:
                break
            dirn = -g/gn; dn=1.0
        else:
            dirn = dirn / dn
        dg = float(np.dot(dirn,g))
        if dg > 0:
            gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
            if gn < 1e-20: break
            dirn = -g/gn
            dg = -float(np.dot(dirn,g))
        a_old = a
        f_old = f
        cmax_old = cmax
        max_change = step_base
        alpha = 1.0
        accepted = False
        cand = a_old
        c_ls = c
        A_ls = A
        cmax_ls = cmax_old
        f_ls = f_old
        best_cand_a = None; best_cand_c = None; best_cand_A = None; best_cand_cmax = inf; best_cand_f = inf
        for ls in range(7):
            cand = project_simplex(a_old + alpha*max_change*dirn, S)
            c_ls, A_ls = compute_conv(cand,N)
            cmax_ls = float(np.max(c_ls))
            w_ls, f_ls, cmax_ls2 = lse_weights_f(c_ls,beta)
            if cmax_ls < best_cand_cmax or (abs(cmax_ls-best_cand_cmax)<=1e-12 and f_ls<best_cand_f):
                best_cand_a=cand; best_cand_c=c_ls; best_cand_A=A_ls; best_cand_cmax=cmax_ls; best_cand_f=f_ls
            if cmax_ls < cmax_old - 1e-10:
                accepted = True
                break
            if f_ls < f_old - max(1e-10, 1e-4*alpha*max_change*max(0.0, -dg)) and cmax_ls < cmax_old + 0.02:
                accepted = True
                break
            alpha *= 0.5
            if alpha < 1e-4:
                break
        if best_cand_a is not None:
            # Use the best candidate if it improves either objective enough, even if loop didn't label accepted
            if best_cand_cmax < cmax_old - 1e-10 or best_cand_f < f_old - 1e-8:
                a = best_cand_a
                c = best_cand_c
                A = best_cand_A
                cmax = best_cand_cmax
                f = best_cand_f
                accepted = True
        if accepted:
            if cmax < best_obj - 1e-12:
                best_obj = cmax; best_a = a.copy(); last_best = cmax
            g_new, w_new, f_new, cmax_new = lse_grad_from_a_cA(a,c,A,N,beta)
            s_new = a - a_old
            y_new = g_new - g
            sy = float(np.dot(s_new,y_new))
            ss = float(np.dot(s_new,s_new))
            if sy > 1e-14*ss and ss > 1e-30:
                s_hist.append(s_new); y_hist.append(y_new)
                if len(s_hist)>m_lbfgs:
                    s_hist.pop(0); y_hist.pop(0)
            g = g_new
            f = f_new
            # update cmax maybe from g? c same
            if last_best > cmax:
                last_best = cmax
            # if small improvements continue, else stall logic
            if it % 200 == 0 and cmax >= last_best - 1e-10:
                stall += 1
            if stall >= 6:
                # moderate restart from best
                a = project_simplex(best_a + rng.normal(0, 0.01*S/np.sqrt(n), n), S)
                c,A = compute_conv(a,N); cmax=float(np.max(c))
                g,w,f,cmax = lse_grad_from_a_cA(a,c,A,N,beta)
                s_hist=[]; y_hist=[]
                step_base = max(1e-6, step_base*1.5)
                stall = 0
        else:
            rejects += 1
            step_base = max(1e-6, step_base*0.6)
            # small random kick
            kick = 0.003*S/np.sqrt(n)
            a = project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0,kick,n), S)
            c,A = compute_conv(a,N); cmax=float(np.max(c))
            if cmax < best_obj - 1e-12:
                best_obj=cmax; best_a=a.copy(); last_best=cmax
            g,w,f,cmax = lse_grad_from_a_cA(a,c,A,N,beta)
            s_hist=[]; y_hist=[]
            if rejects > 5 or (it % 300 == 0 and cmax >= last_best -1e-10):
                stall += 1
                if stall >= 4:
                    # restart from best with larger kick
                    a = project_simplex(best_a + rng.normal(0,0.02*S/np.sqrt(n),n),S)
                    c,A=compute_conv(a,N); cmax=float(np.max(c))
                    g,w,f,cmax = lse_grad_from_a_cA(a,c,A,N,beta)
                    beta = min(float(beta_max), max(float(beta_start), beta*1.5))
                    s_hist=[]; y_hist=[]
                    step_base = 2.0*S/(n*max(beta,1.0))
                    stall=0; rejects=0
    return best_a, best_obj
```

```
    a=safe_normalize(a_init,n,S)
    c,A=compute_conv(a,N); cmax=float(np.max(c))
```

Detailed code drafts:

`project_simplex`:
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if S <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cs = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    valid = (cs - S) <= (w * idx)
    if valid.size > 0:
        vals = np.where(valid)[0]
        r = int(vals[-1]) + 1
    else:
        r = n
    theta = (cs[r - 1] - S) / float(r)
    u = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(u))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        u = np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        u *= S / s
    return u
```

`safe_normalize`:
```
def safe_normalize(a,n,S):
    a = clean_array(a)
    if a.size != n:
       if a.size ==0:
          a = np.zeros(n)
       else:
          a = np.resize(a,n)
    a = np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    return project_simplex(a,S)
```
If resize repeats, maybe not desired but only for wrong lengths. For interpolation used before.

`compute_conv`:

```
def compute_conv(a,N):
    A=fft(a,N)
    c=np.real(ifft(A*A))[:2*a.size-1]
    return c,A
```

`init_lowfreq`: Need avoid frequencies zero. For n large powers. Code:
```
def init_lowfreq(n,rng):
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.ones(n,dtype=np.float64)
    pairs=[(n//2,0.65),(n//3,0.25),(n//5,0.15),(n//7,0.10),(n//11,0.08),(n//13,0.06)]
    for f,amp in pairs:
        if f < 1: continue
        a = a + amp*np.cos((2.0*np.pi*i*f)/float(n) + rng.random()*2.0*np.pi)
    a = a + 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
    return np.clip(a,0.01,None)
```
For f=n//2, term cos(π i + phase). If phase not 0, not exactly alternating but period n/gcd? gcd(n/2,n)=n/2, period2 still with phase. Good.

`init_fourier`:
```
def init_fourier(n,rng,N):
    P = rng.exponential(1.0, N)
    P[0] = float(n)
    P = np.maximum(P, 1e-6)
    phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi, N)
    A = np.sqrt(P) * np.exp(1j*phase)
    a = np.real(ifft(A))[:n]
    return np.abs(a) + 0.01
```
Good.

`factor_candidates`: If Q has zero? sqrt. If A mid? okay.
```
def factor_candidates(c,n,S,N,rng,flip_p=0.7):
    Q = fft(c,N)
    Q = make_conj_sym(Q)
    Q[0] = max(0.0, float(Q[0].real))
    if N % 2 == 0:
        Q[N//2] = abs(Q[N//2])
    A = np.sqrt(Q)
    out=[]
    t = np.real(ifft(A))
    s = best_window_start(t,n)
    seg = get_window(t,s,n)
    out.append(project_simplex(np.maximum(seg,0.0),S))
    out.append(project_simplex(np.abs(seg),S))
    if flip_p > 0:
        A2 = apply_random_flips(A,rng,flip_p)
        t2 = np.real(ifft(A2))
        s2 = best_window_start(t2,n)
        seg2 = get_window(t2,s2,n)
        out.append(project_simplex(np.maximum(seg2,0.0),S))
        out.append(project_simplex(np.abs(seg2),S))
    return out
```
Potential `np.sqrt` on Q with large negative? complex. okay.

`make_conj_sym` for N even: implementation as above. Need `q` maybe not complex? It is complex from fft. Use `np.empty_like(q)`, dtype complex. For slices, if mid=1, out[1:1] okay. `out[mid+1:]` length? For N=2, mid=1, out[2:] empty, q[0:0:-1]? `q[mid-1:0:-1]` with mid-1=0 -> empty. okay.
For N even, what about out[N? no]. Good.

`apply_random_flips` for N even:
```
def apply_random_flips(A,rng,p):
    N=A.size
    if p <= 0.0 or N < 4:
        return A
    A2 = A.copy()
    if N % 2 == 0:
        end = N//2
    else:
        end = N//2 + 1
    idx = np.arange(1, end)
    if idx.size:
        flips = rng.random(idx.size) < p
        k = idx[flips]
        if k.size:
            A2[k] = -A2[k]
            A2[N-k] = -A2[N-k]
    return A2
```
If N-k maybe equals k? For even, k<mid so N-k>k; for odd, max k=mid, N-k=mid? For N odd, mid=(N-1)/2, k=mid, N-k=mid+1? Wait if N=5, pairs (1,4),(2,3); end=N//2+1=3, idx [1,2], N-k [4,3], okay. Good.

`best_window_start` uses `np.full`? code:
```
def best_window_start(t,n):
    N=t.size
    if n <=0 or N <= n: return 0
    tt=np.empty(2*N,dtype=np.float64)
    tt[:N]=t; tt[N:]=t
    ps=np.empty(2*N+1,dtype=np.float64)
    ps[0]=0.0
    ps[1:] = np.cumsum(tt)
    vals = ps[n:n+N] - ps[:N]
    return int(np.argmax(vals)) % N
```
If N large and called many, okay.

`get_window`:
```
def get_window(t,s,n):
    N=t.size
    seg=np.empty(n,dtype=np.float64)
    if s == 0:
        seg[:] = t[:n]
    elif s + n <= N:
        seg[:] = t[s:s+n]
    else:
        first = N - s
        seg[:first] = t[s:]
        seg[first:] = t[:s+n-N]
    return seg
```
If s+n-N could be 0 if exactly N? works.

`cap_redistribute`: Need ensure `c.size` not zero. n large. Code:
```
def cap_redistribute(c,S2,L):
    c = np.array(c, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    size = c.size
    if size == 0: return c
    avg = S2 / float(size)
    L = max(float(L), avg + 1e-12)
    c = np.clip(c, 0.0, L)
    for _ in range(8):
        s = float(np.sum(c))
        if s <= 1e-15:
            c.fill(np.minimum(1000.0, avg))  # avg gets clipped? if avg>L impossible but L>=avg.
            break
        diff = S2 - s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-8 * max(1.0, S2):
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = L - c
            room = np.maximum(room, 0.0)
            rsum = float(np.sum(room))
            if rsum <= 1e-15:
                c.fill(min(avg, L))
                break
            c += diff * room / rsum
        else:
            c *= S2 / s
        c = np.clip(c, 0.0, L)
    s=float(np.sum(c))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        c.fill(min(avg, L))
    elif s != S2:
        # final proportional adjust if possible
        c = c * (S2/s)
        c = np.clip(c,0.0,L)
        # if scaled down sum lower due clip? add room
        s=float(np.sum(c)); diff=S2-s
        if diff>0:
           room=L-c; room=np.maximum(room,0); rsum=room.sum()
           if rsum>0: c += diff*room/rsum
        elif diff<0:
           c *= S2/max(s,1e-300); c=np.clip(c,0,L)
    return c
```
The final adjust can loop? We can keep simple; small diffs fine. The objective sum internal c from a equals S2; target sum slight errors affect factor A[0] slightly, but safe_normalize a to S. okay.

`make_base_target`:
```
def make_base_target(n,S2,L,rng,amp=0.0):
    L = (L if L is not None else 1.5)
    size = 2*n - 1
    L = max(float(L), S2/float(size) + 1e-12)
    kk = np.arange(size, dtype=np.float64)
    tri = np.minimum(kk + 1.0, float(size) - kk)
    tri *= S2 / float(n*n)
    c = np.minimum(tri, L)
    if amp > 0 and rng is not None:
        c = c + rng.normal(0.0, amp*L, size)
    return cap_redistribute(c,S2,L)
```
`float(size) - kk`: for kk=0,size; for kk=size-1,1. Good.

```
def target_candidates(a,n,S,N,c_current,L,rng,use_base,flip_p=0.7):
    S2=S*S
    avg=S2/(2*n-1)
    L=max(float(L), avg+1e-12)
    if use_base:
       ctarget=make_base_target(n,S2,L,rng,amp=0.05)
    else:
       ctarget=cap_redistribute(c_current,S2,L)
       if rng is not None and rng.random()<0.35:
          ctarget2 = ctarget + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02*L, ctarget.size)
          ctarget = cap_redistribute(ctarget2,S2,L)
    return factor_candidates(ctarget,n,S,N,rng,flip_p)
```

```
def build_inits(n,S,N,rng,given_seq,extra_seq,need_spectral=True):
    inits=[]
    inits.append(np.full(n,S/float(n),dtype=np.float64))
    inits.append(init_rand(n,rng))
    inits.append(init_chirp(n,rng))
    inits.append(init_lowfreq(n,rng))
    inits.append(init_fourier(n,rng,N))
    if need_spectral:
        inits.append(init_spectral_target(n,S,N,rng,1.50))
    if given_seq is not None:
        inits.append(interpolate_seq(given_seq,n))
    if extra_seq is not None:
        inits.append(interpolate_seq(extra_seq,n))
    return inits
```
If given/extra None. Good.

```
def optimize_length(n,time_budget,rng,given_seq,extra_seq):
   if time_budget <=0 or n<8:
       S=np.sqrt(2.0*float(max(n,1))); return np.full(max(n,1),S/float(max(n,1))),2.0
   S=...; N=...

   t_start=time.monotonic()
   t_end=t_start+time_budget
   print(...)
   inits=build_inits(...)
   # evaluate inits
   for arr in inits:
      a_init=safe_normalize(arr,n,S)
      c,A=compute_conv(a_init,N); obj=float(np.max(c))
      if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
          best_a=a_init.copy(); best_obj=obj

   phase1_budget = min(0.18*time_budget, 260.0) if time_budget>200 else 0.25*time_budget
   phase1_budget = max(0.0, min(phase1_budget, t_end - time.monotonic() - 30.0))
   if phase1_budget > 10:
       per = phase1_budget / max(1,len(inits))
       phase1_end = time.monotonic() + phase1_budget
       for arr in inits:
          rem = phase1_end - time.monotonic()
          if rem < 1.0: break
          a_init=safe_normalize(arr,n,S)
          block_time = min(per*1.2, rem*0.95, 90.0)
          if block_time < 1.0: continue
          a_res,obj_res = lbfgs_block(a_init,n,S,N,block_time,rng,4.0,90.0,8)
          if obj_res < best_obj -1e-12:
              best_a=a_res.copy(); best_obj=obj_res; print
              if time.monotonic() > phase1_end: break
   # phase2
   remaining = t_end - time.monotonic()
   phase2_budget = min(0.45*time_budget, max(0.0, remaining - 80.0))
   if phase2_budget > 10:
      t2_end = time.monotonic() + phase2_budget
      a_curr = best_a.copy()
      stall=0
      while time.monotonic() < t2_end - 2:
         block_time = min(120.0, t2_end - time.monotonic())
         if block_time < 5: break
         beta_s = 8.0 + 4.0*min(stall,12)
         beta_e = min(500.0, 50.0 + 25.0*stall)
         a_res,obj_res=lbfgs_block(a_curr,n,S,N,block_time,rng,beta_s,beta_e,12)
         if obj_res < best_obj - 1e-10:
             best_a=a_res.copy(); best_obj=obj_res; a_curr=best_a.copy(); stall=0
             print(" phase2 n=%d best=%.6f" % (n,best_obj))
         else:
             stall += 1
             a_curr = project_simplex(best_a + rng.normal(0, 0.01*S/np.sqrt(n), n), S)
             if stall >= 4:
                c,A=compute_conv(a_curr,N)
                cands=target_candidates(a_curr,n,S,N,c,max(1.46,best_obj-0.05),rng,True,0.7)
                jump_a=None; jump_obj=inf
                for cand in cands:
                   c2,_=compute_conv(cand,N); obj=float(np.max(c2))
                   if obj < jump_obj: jump_obj=obj; jump_a=cand
                if jump_obj < best_obj -1e-10:
                   best_a=jump_a.copy(); best_obj=jump_obj; a_curr=best_a.copy()
                   print(" phase2 jump %.6f" % best_obj)
                elif jump_a is not None:
                   a_curr=jump_a
                stall=0
   # phase3
   remaining = t_end - time.monotonic()
   phase3_budget = min(0.20*time_budget, max(0.0, remaining - 30.0))
   if phase3_budget > 8:
      a_sp,obj_sp=spectral_block(best_a,n,S,N,phase3_budget,rng,1.46,1.52)
      if obj_sp < best_obj -1e-12:
         best_a=a_sp.copy(); best_obj=obj_sp; print
   # phase4

   phase4_budget = min(0.20*time_budget, max(0.0, remaining - 5.0))
   if phase4_budget > 8:
      a_fin,obj_fin=lbfgs_block(best_a,n,S,N,phase4_budget,rng,50.0,600.0,10)

         best_a=a_sp2.copy(); best_obj=obj_sp2; print
   print(" finish n=%d best=%.6f" % (n,best_obj))
   return best_a,best_obj
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative step sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The code normalizes sequences to sum sqrt(2n), so the objective is the
maximum coefficient of the linear self-convolution.  It combines:
  1. Multi-start L-BFGS on an LSE-smoothed max-convolution objective.
  2. Direct convolution-domain target shaping followed by spectral
     factorization (square root of the DFT of a capped/redistributed target).
  3. Adaptive spectral and local L-BFGS polishing.
  4. Optionally, lengths 8192 and 16384, keeping the best found.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft


def clean_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = a.copy()
    bad = ~np.isfinite(a)
    if np.any(bad):
        a[bad] = 0.0
    return a


def project_simplex(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if S <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cs = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    valid = (cs - S) <= (w * idx)

    if valid.size > 0:
        vals = np.where(valid)[0]
        r = int(vals[-1]) + 1
    else:
        r = n

    theta = (cs[r - 1] - S) / float(r)
    u = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(u))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        u = np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        u *= S / s
    return u


def safe_normalize(a, n, S):
    a = clean_array(a)
    if a.size != n:
        if a.size == 0:
            a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            a = np.resize(a, n)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return project_simplex(a, S)


def compute_conv(a, N):
    A = fft(a, N)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))[:2 * a.size - 1]
    return c, A


def lse_weights_f(c, beta):
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if beta <= 1e-12:
        w = np.ones_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
        if w.size:
            w /= float(w.size)
        return w, cmax, cmax

    diff = np.minimum(beta * (c - cmax), 0.0)
    e = np.exp(diff)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s < 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
        if c.size:
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        f = cmax
    else:
        w = e / s
        f = cmax + np.log(s) / beta
    return w, f, cmax


def lse_grad_from_a_cA(a, c, A, N, beta):
    w, f, cmax = lse_weights_f(c, beta)
    W = fft(w, N)
    grad = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:a.size]
    return grad, w, f, cmax


def lse_grad(a, N, beta):
    c, A = compute_conv(a, N)
    return lse_grad_from_a_cA(a, c, A, N, beta)


def lbfgs_dir(s_hist, y_hist, g):
    m = len(s_hist)
    n = len(g)
    gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))

    if m == 0 or gn < 1e-300:
        if gn < 1e-300:
            return np.zeros(n)
        return -g / gn

    q = g.copy()
    alphas = np.zeros(m)
    for i in range(m - 1, -1, -1):
        s_i = s_hist[i]
        y_i = y_hist[i]
        yy = float(np.dot(y_i, y_i))
        if yy < 1e-30:
            alphas[i] = 0.0
        else:
            alphas[i] = float(np.dot(s_i, q)) / yy
            q = q - alphas[i] * y_i

    s_last = s_hist[-1]
    y_last = y_hist[-1]
    yy_last = float(np.dot(y_last, y_last))
    if yy_last > 1e-30:
        gamma = max(1e-12, float(np.dot(s_last, y_last)) / yy_last)
    else:
        gamma = 1.0

    r = gamma * q
    for i in range(m):
        s_i = s_hist[i]
        y_i = y_hist[i]
        yy = float(np.dot(y_i, y_i))
        if yy < 1e-30:
            continue
        beta_i = float(np.dot(s_i, y_i)) / yy
        r = r + (alphas[i] - beta_i) * y_i

    direction = -r
    if float(np.dot(g, direction)) > 0:
        direction = -g
    dn = float(np.linalg.norm(direction))
    if dn < 1e-20:
        if gn < 1e-20:
            return np.zeros(n)
        direction = -g / gn
    return direction


def lbfgs_block(a_init, n, S, N, max_time, rng,
                beta_start=5.0, beta_max=200.0, m_lbfgs=10):
    a = safe_normalize(a_init, n, S)
    c, A = compute_conv(a, N)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = cmax

    if max_time <= 0:
        return best_a, best_obj

    t_end = time.monotonic() + max_time
    beta = float(beta_start)
    g, w, f, cmax = lse_grad_from_a_cA(a, c, A, N, beta)
    s_hist = []
    y_hist = []
    step_base = 2.0 * S / (n * max(beta, 1.0))

    last_best = best_obj
    stall = 0
    rejects = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        it += 1

        if it % 100 == 0:
            beta = min(float(beta_max), beta * 1.06)
            step_base = 2.0 * S / (n * max(beta, 1.0))
            s_hist = []
            y_hist = []
            g, w, f, cmax = lse_grad_from_a_cA(a, c, A, N, beta)
            if cmax < best_obj - 1e-12:
                best_obj = cmax
                best_a = a.copy()
                last_best = cmax

        dirn = lbfgs_dir(s_hist, y_hist, g)
        dn = float(np.linalg.norm(dirn))
        if dn < 1e-20:
            gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
            if gn < 1e-20:
                break
            dirn = -g / gn
            dn = 1.0
        else:
            dirn = dirn / dn

        dg = float(np.dot(dirn, g))
        if dg > 0:
            gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
            if gn < 1e-20:
                break
            dirn = -g / gn
            dg = -float(np.dot(dirn, g))

        a_old = a
        f_old = f
        cmax_old = cmax
        max_change = step_base
        alpha = 1.0
        accepted = False

        best_cand_a = None
        best_cand_c = None
        best_cand_A = None
        best_cand_cmax = float("inf")
        best_cand_f = float("inf")

        for ls in range(7):
            cand = project_simplex(a_old + alpha * max_change * dirn, S)
            c_ls, A_ls = compute_conv(cand, N)
            cmax_ls = float(np.max(c_ls))
            w_ls, f_ls, cmax_ls2 = lse_weights_f(c_ls, beta)

            if cmax_ls < best_cand_cmax or (
                abs(cmax_ls - best_cand_cmax) <= 1e-12 and f_ls < best_cand_f
            ):
                best_cand_a = cand
                best_cand_c = c_ls
                best_cand_A = A_ls
                best_cand_cmax = cmax_ls
                best_cand_f = f_ls

            if cmax_ls < cmax_old - 1e-10:
                accepted = True
                break

            if f_ls < f_old - max(
                1e-10,
                1e-4 * alpha * max_change * max(0.0, -dg)
            ) and cmax_ls < cmax_old + 0.02:
                accepted = True
                break

            alpha *= 0.5
            if alpha < 1e-4:
                break

        if best_cand_a is not None and (
            best_cand_cmax < cmax_old - 1e-10 or best_cand_f < f_old - 1e-8
        ):
            a = best_cand_a
            c = best_cand_c
            A = best_cand_A
            cmax = best_cand_cmax
            f = best_cand_f
            accepted = True

        if accepted:
            if cmax < best_obj - 1e-12:
                best_obj = cmax
                best_a = a.copy()
            last_best = min(last_best, cmax)

            g_new, w_new, f_new, cmax_new = lse_grad_from_a_cA(a, c, A, N, beta)
            s_new = a - a_old
            y_new = g_new - g
            sy = float(np.dot(s_new, y_new))
            ss = float(np.dot(s_new, s_new))
            if sy > 1e-14 * ss and ss > 1e-30:
                s_hist.append(s_new)
                y_hist.append(y_new)
                if len(s_hist) > m_lbfgs:
                    s_hist.pop(0)
                    y_hist.pop(0)

            g = g_new
            f = f_new

            if it % 200 == 0 and cmax >= last_best - 1e-10:
                stall += 1
            if stall >= 6:
                a = project_simplex(
                    best_a + rng.normal(0, 0.01 * S / np.sqrt(n), n), S
                )
                c, A = compute_conv(a, N)
                cmax = float(np.max(c))
                g, w, f, cmax = lse_grad_from_a_cA(a, c, A, N, beta)
                s_hist = []
                y_hist = []
                step_base = max(1e-6, step_base * 1.5)
                stall = 0
                rejects = 0
        else:
            rejects += 1
            step_base = max(1e-6, step_base * 0.6)
            a = project_simplex(
                a + rng.normal(0, 0.003 * S / np.sqrt(n), n), S
            )
            c, A = compute_conv(a, N)
            cmax = float(np.max(c))
            if cmax < best_obj - 1e-12:
                best_obj = cmax
                best_a = a.copy()
            g, w, f, cmax = lse_grad_from_a_cA(a, c, A, N, beta)
            s_hist = []
            y_hist = []

            if rejects > 5 or (it % 300 == 0 and cmax >= last_best - 1e-10):
                stall += 1
                if stall >= 4:
                    a = project_simplex(
                        best_a + rng.normal(0, 0.02 * S / np.sqrt(n), n), S
                    )
                    c, A = compute_conv(a, N)
                    cmax = float(np.max(c))
                    g, w, f, cmax = lse_grad_from_a_cA(a, c, A, N, beta)
                    beta = min(float(beta_max), max(float(beta_start), beta * 1.5))
                    s_hist = []
                    y_hist = []
                    step_base = 2.0 * S / (n * max(beta, 1.0))
                    stall = 0
                    rejects = 0

    return best_a, best_obj


def make_conj_sym(q):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.complex128)
    out = np.empty_like(q)
    N = q.size
    if N == 0:
        return out
    if N % 2 == 0:
        mid = N // 2
        out[0] = q[0].real
        out[mid] = q[mid].real
        if mid > 1:
            out[1:mid] = q[1:mid]
            out[mid + 1:] = np.conj(q[mid - 1:0:-1])
    else:
        mid = (N - 1) // 2
        out[0] = q[0].real
        out[1:mid + 1] = q[1:mid + 1]
        out[mid + 1:] = np.conj(q[mid:0:-1])
    return out


def apply_random_flips(A, rng, p):
    N = A.size
    if p <= 0.0 or N < 4:
        return A
    A2 = A.copy()
    if N % 2 == 0:
        end = N // 2
    else:
        end = N // 2 + 1
    idx = np.arange(1, end)
    if idx.size:
        flips = rng.random(idx.size) < p
        k = idx[flips]
        if k.size:
            A2[k] = -A2[k]
            A2[N - k] = -A2[N - k]
    return A2


def best_window_start(t, n):
    N = t.size
    if n <= 0 or N <= n:
        return 0
    tt = np.empty(2 * N, dtype=np.float64)
    tt[:N] = t
    tt[N:] = t
    ps = np.empty(2 * N + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    ps[0] = 0.0
    ps[1:] = np.cumsum(tt)
    vals = ps[n:n + N] - ps[:N]
    return int(np.argmax(vals)) % N


def get_window(t, s, n):
    N = t.size
    seg = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if s == 0:
        seg[:] = t[:n]
    elif s + n <= N:
        seg[:] = t[s:s + n]
    else:
        first = N - s
        seg[:first] = t[s:]
        seg[first:] = t[:s + n - N]
    return seg


def factor_candidates(c, n, S, N, rng, flip_p=0.7):
    Q = fft(c, N)
    Q = make_conj_sym(Q)
    Q[0] = max(0.0, float(Q[0].real))
    if N % 2 == 0:
        Q[N // 2] = abs(Q[N // 2])

    A = np.sqrt(Q)

    out = []
    t = np.real(ifft(A))
    s = best_window_start(t, n)
    seg = get_window(t, s, n)
    out.append(project_simplex(np.maximum(seg, 0.0), S))
    out.append(project_simplex(np.abs(seg), S))

    if flip_p > 0:
        A2 = apply_random_flips(A, rng, flip_p)
        t2 = np.real(ifft(A2))
        s2 = best_window_start(t2, n)
        seg2 = get_window(t2, s2, n)
        out.append(project_simplex(np.maximum(seg2, 0.0), S))
        out.append(project_simplex(np.abs(seg2), S))

    return out


def cap_redistribute(c, S2, L):
    c = np.array(c, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    size = c.size
    if size == 0:
        return c

    avg = S2 / float(size)
    L = max(float(L), avg + 1e-12)
    c = np.clip(c, 0.0, L)

    for _ in range(8):
        s = float(np.sum(c))
        if s <= 1e-15:
            c.fill(min(avg, L))
            break
        diff = S2 - s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-8 * max(1.0, S2):
            break

        if diff > 0:
            room = L - c
            room = np.maximum(room, 0.0)
            rsum = float(np.sum(room))
            if rsum <= 1e-15:
                c.fill(min(avg, L))
                break
            c += diff * room / rsum
        else:
            c *= S2 / s
        c = np.clip(c, 0.0, L)

    s = float(np.sum(c))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        c.fill(min(avg, L))
    elif abs(s - S2) > 1e-8 * max(1.0, S2):
        c = c * (S2 / s)
        c = np.clip(c, 0.0, L)
        s2 = float(np.sum(c))
        diff = S2 - s2
        if diff > 0:
            room = L - c
            room = np.maximum(room, 0.0)
            rsum = float(np.sum(room))
            if rsum > 0:
                c += diff * room / rsum
        elif diff < 0:
            c *= S2 / max(s2, 1e-300)
            c = np.clip(c, 0.0, L)

    return c


def make_base_target(n, S2, L, rng, amp=0.0):
    size = 2 * n - 1
    L = max(float(L), S2 / float(size) + 1e-12)

    kk = np.arange(size, dtype=np.float64)
    tri = np.minimum(kk + 1.0, float(size) - kk)
    tri *= S2 / float(n * n)

    c = np.minimum(tri, L)
    if amp > 0 and rng is not None:
        c = c + rng.normal(0.0, amp * L, size)
    return cap_redistribute(c, S2, L)


def target_candidates(a, n, S, N, c_current, L, rng, use_base, flip_p=0.7):
    S2 = S * S
    avg = S2 / float(2 * n - 1)
    L = max(float(L), avg + 1e-12)

    if use_base:
        ctarget = make_base_target(n, S2, L, rng, amp=0.05)
    else:
        ctarget = cap_redistribute(c_current, S2, L)
        if rng is not None and rng.random() < 0.35:
            ctarget = ctarget + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02 * L, ctarget.size)
            ctarget = cap_redistribute(ctarget, S2, L)

    return factor_candidates(ctarget, n, S, N, rng, flip_p)


def spectral_block(a_init, n, S, N, max_time, rng,
                   target_lower=1.44, target_upper=1.52):
    a = safe_normalize(a_init, n, S)
    c, A = compute_conv(a, N)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = cmax

    if max_time <= 0:
        return best_a, best_obj

    t_end = time.monotonic() + max_time
    S2 = S * S
    avg = S2 / float(2 * n - 1)
    L = min(float(target_upper), max(float(target_lower), cmax - 0.04))
    L = max(L, avg + 1e-12)

    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        it += 1
        use_base = (stall >= 3) or (rng.random() < 0.15)
        cands = target_candidates(a, n, S, N, c, L, rng, use_base, 0.7)
        if not cands:
            break

        best_cand = None
        best_cand_obj = float("inf")
        best_cand_c = None

        for cand in cands:
            c2, A2 = compute_conv(cand, N)
            obj = float(np.max(c2))
            if obj < best_cand_obj:
                best_cand_obj = obj
                best_cand = cand
                best_cand_c = c2

        if best_cand is None:
            break

        if best_cand_obj < best_obj - 1e-10:
            best_obj = best_cand_obj
            best_a = best_cand.copy()
            a = best_cand.copy()
            c = best_cand_c.copy()
            cmax = best_cand_obj
            L = max(float(target_lower), min(float(target_upper), cmax - 0.025))
            L = max(L, avg + 1e-12)
            stall = 0
        elif best_cand_obj < cmax - 1e-10:
            a = best_cand.copy()
            c = best_cand_c.copy()
            cmax = best_cand_obj
            L = max(float(target_lower), min(float(target_upper), cmax - 0.015))
            L = max(L, avg + 1e-12)
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            L = min(min(cmax, float(target_upper)), L + 0.02 + 0.01 * stall)
            if L >= cmax:
                L = max(float(target_lower), cmax - 0.01)
            L = max(L, avg + 1e-12)

            if stall > 10:
                a = project_simplex(
                    a + rng.normal(0, 0.005 * S / np.sqrt(n), n), S
                )
                c, A = compute_conv(a, N)
                cmax = float(np.max(c))
                if cmax < best_obj - 1e-12:
                    best_obj = cmax
                    best_a = a.copy()
                L = max(float(target_lower), cmax - 0.05)
                L = max(L, avg + 1e-12)
                stall = 0

    return best_a, best_obj


def init_rand(n, rng):
    return rng.random(n) + 0.05


def init_chirp(n, rng):
    tt = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    a = 0.6 + 0.4 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * (3.0 * tt + tt * tt))
    a = a + 0.1 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    return np.clip(a, 0.01, None)


def init_lowfreq(n, rng):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    pairs = [
        (n // 2, 0.65),
        (n // 3, 0.25),
        (n // 5, 0.15),
        (n // 7, 0.10),
        (n // 11, 0.08),
        (n // 13, 0.06),
    ]
    for f, amp in pairs:
        if f < 1:
            continue
        a = a + amp * np.cos((2.0 * np.pi * i * f) / float(n) + rng.random() * 2.0 * np.pi)
    a = a + 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    return np.clip(a, 0.01, None)


def init_fourier(n, rng, N):
    P = rng.exponential(1.0, N)
    P[0] = float(n)
    P = np.maximum(P, 1e-6)
    phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, N)
    A = np.sqrt(P) * np.exp(1j * phase)
    a = np.real(ifft(A))[:n]
    return np.abs(a) + 0.01


def interpolate_seq(seq, n):
    seq = clean_array(seq)
    if seq.size == n:
        return seq.copy()
    if seq.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if seq.size < 2:
        return np.full(n, float(seq[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, seq.size)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(new, old, seq)


def init_spectral_target(n, S, N, rng, L=1.50):
    S2 = S * S
    c = make_base_target(n, S2, L, rng, amp=0.05)
    cands = factor_candidates(c, n, S, N, rng, 0.7)

    best_a = np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    best_obj = 2.0

    for cand in cands:
        c2, A2 = compute_conv(cand, N)
        obj = float(np.max(c2))
        if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_obj = obj
            best_a = cand.copy()

    return best_a


def build_inits(n, S, N, rng, given_seq, extra_seq, need_spectral=True):
    inits = []
    inits.append(np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64))
    inits.append(init_rand(n, rng))
    inits.append(init_chirp(n, rng))
    inits.append(init_lowfreq(n, rng))
    inits.append(init_fourier(n, rng, N))

    if need_spectral:
        inits.append(init_spectral_target(n, S, N, rng, 1.50))

    if given_seq is not None:
        inits.append(interpolate_seq(given_seq, n))
    if extra_seq is not None:
        inits.append(interpolate_seq(extra_seq, n))

    return inits


def optimize_length(n, time_budget, rng, given_seq, extra_seq):
    if n < 8:
        n = max(8, int(n))

    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    N = 1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length()

    a_uni = np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    best_a = a_uni.copy()
    best_obj = 2.0

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    t_end = t_start + max(0.0, time_budget)
    print("optimize n=%d budget=%.1fs FFTN=%d" % (n, time_budget, N))

    inits = build_inits(n, S, N, rng, given_seq, extra_seq, need_spectral=time_budget > 300)

    # Quick initialization evaluation.
    for arr in inits:
        a_init = safe_normalize(arr, n, S)
        c, A = compute_conv(a_init, N)
        obj = float(np.max(c))
        if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_obj = obj
            best_a = a_init.copy()
            print("  init obj=%.6f" % best_obj)

    # Phase 1: short optimization from many starts.
    if time_budget > 200:
        phase1_budget = min(0.18 * time_budget, 260.0)
    else:
        phase1_budget = 0.25 * time_budget
    phase1_budget = max(0.0, min(phase1_budget, t_end - time.monotonic() - 30.0))

    if phase1_budget > 10:
        per = phase1_budget / max(1, len(inits))
        phase1_end = time.monotonic() + phase1_budget
        for arr in inits:
            rem = phase1_end - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 1.0:
                break
            a_init = safe_normalize(arr, n, S)
            block_time = min(per * 1.2, rem * 0.95, 90.0)
            if block_time < 1.0:
                continue
            a_res, obj_res = lbfgs_block(a_init, n, S, N, block_time, rng, 4.0, 90.0, 8)
            if obj_res < best_obj - 1e-12:
                best_obj = obj_res
                best_a = a_res.copy()
                print("  phase1 obj=%.6f" % best_obj)

    # Phase 2: longer local optimization with restarts.
    remaining = t_end - time.monotonic()
    phase2_budget = min(0.45 * time_budget, max(0.0, remaining - 80.0))
    if phase2_budget > 10:
        t2_end = time.monotonic() + phase2_budget
        a_curr = best_a.copy()
        stall = 0

        while time.monotonic() < t2_end - 2.0:
            block_time = min(120.0, t2_end - time.monotonic())
            if block_time < 5.0:
                break
            beta_s = 8.0 + 4.0 * min(stall, 12)
            beta_e = min(500.0, 50.0 + 25.0 * stall)
            a_res, obj_res = lbfgs_block(a_curr, n, S, N, block_time, rng,
                                         beta_s, beta_e, 12)
            if obj_res < best_obj - 1e-10:
                best_obj = obj_res
                best_a = a_res.copy()
                a_curr = best_a.copy()
                stall = 0
                print("  phase2 obj=%.6f" % best_obj)
            else:
                stall += 1
                a_curr = project_simplex(
                    best_a + rng.normal(0, 0.01 * S / np.sqrt(n), n), S
                )

                if stall >= 4:
                    c, A = compute_conv(a_curr, N)
                    cands = target_candidates(
                        a_curr, n, S, N, c, max(1.46, best_obj - 0.05), rng, True, 0.7
                    )
                    jump_a = None
                    jump_obj = float("inf")
                    for cand in cands:
                        c2, A2 = compute_conv(cand, N)
                        obj = float(np.max(c2))
                        if obj < jump_obj:
                            jump_obj = obj
                            jump_a = cand

                    if jump_obj < best_obj - 1e-10:
                        best_obj = jump_obj
                        best_a = jump_a.copy()
                        a_curr = best_a.copy()
                        print("  phase2 jump obj=%.6f" % best_obj)
                    elif jump_a is not None:
                        a_curr = jump_a.copy()
                    stall = 0

    # Phase 3: direct spectral target shaping.
    remaining = t_end - time.monotonic()
    phase3_budget = min(0.20 * time_budget, max(0.0, remaining - 30.0))
    if phase3_budget > 8:
        a_sp, obj_sp = spectral_block(best_a, n, S, N, phase3_budget, rng, 1.44, 1.52)
        if obj_sp < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_obj = obj_sp
            best_a = a_sp.copy()
            print("  phase3 spectral obj=%.6f" % best_obj)

    # Phase 4: high-beta L-BFGS polishing.
    remaining = t_end - time.monotonic()
    phase4_budget = min(0.20 * time_budget, max(0.0, remaining - 5.0))
    if phase4_budget > 8:
        a_fin, obj_fin = lbfgs_block(best_a, n, S, N, phase4_budget, rng, 50.0, 600.0, 10)
        if obj_fin < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_obj = obj_fin
            best_a = a_fin.copy()
            print("  phase4 polish obj=%.6f" % best_obj)

    # Phase 5: final short spectral attempt.
    rem = t_end - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 15:
        a_sp2, obj_sp2 = spectral_block(
            best_a, n, S, N, min(rem - 4.0, 120.0), rng,
            1.44, min(1.52, best_obj + 0.02)
        )
        if obj_sp2 < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_obj = obj_sp2
            best_a = a_sp2.copy()
            print("  phase5 spectral obj=%.6f" % best_obj)

    print("  finish n=%d best=%.6f" % (n, best_obj))
    return best_a, best_obj


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    usable = max(10.0, budget_s - 20.0)

    given_seq = None
    try:
        given_seq = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if given_seq.size < 1:
            given_seq = None
    except Exception:
        given_seq = None

    best_a = None
    best_obj = float("inf")
    best_len = 0

    if usable >= 1200:
        lengths = [(8192, 0.58 * usable), (16384, 0.42 * usable)]
    elif usable >= 600:
        lengths = [(8192, usable)]
    else:
        lengths = [(2048, usable)]

    if given_seq is not None and 4096 <= given_seq.size <= 16384:
        glen = int(given_seq.size)
        if usable >= 1200:
            if glen == 8192:
                lengths = [(8192, 0.58 * usable), (16384, 0.42 * usable)]
            elif glen == 16384:
                lengths = [(16384, 0.58 * usable), (8192, 0.42 * usable)]
            else:
                lengths = [(glen, 0.58 * usable), (16384, 0.42 * usable)]
        else:
            lengths = [(glen, usable)]

    if len(lengths) == 2:
        b1 = min(lengths[0][1], usable - 200.0)
        b2 = min(lengths[1][1], usable - b1 - 10.0)
        b1 = max(60.0, b1)
        b2 = max(60.0, b2)
        if b1 + b2 > usable - 20.0:
            scale = (usable - 20.0) / (b1 + b2)
            b1 *= scale
            b2 *= scale
        lengths = [(lengths[0][0], b1), (lengths[1][0], b2)]

    deadline = time.monotonic() + usable
    for n, bud in lengths:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0
        t_alloc = min(float(bud), rem)
        if t_alloc <= 20.0:
            continue

        n = int(n)
        a_res, obj_res = optimize_length(n, t_alloc, rng, given_seq, best_a)
        if obj_res < best_obj:
            best_obj = obj_res
            best_a = a_res.copy()
            best_len = n
            print("best after length %d: %.6f" % (n, best_obj))

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.full(256, 1.0 / 256.0, dtype=np.float64)
        best_len = 256
    else:
        # Final rescale to unit sum.  The objective is scale invariant, and
        # unit sum keeps the final values safely away from the 1000 cap.
        total = float(np.sum(best_a))
        if total > 1e-15:
            best_a = best_a * (1.0 / total)
        else:
            best_a = np.full(best_len if best_len > 0 else 256, 0.01)
            best_len = best_a.size

    lst = []
    for x in best_a:
        y = float(x)
        if not np.isfinite(y):
            y = 0.0
        if y < 0.0:
            y = 0.0
        elif y > 1000.0:
            y = 1000.0
        lst.append(y)

    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(lst)
    except Exception:
        val = np.inf

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        lst = [1.0 / 256.0] * 256

    print("final length=%d final_score=%s" % (len(lst), val))
    return lst
```
